# Amsterdam run 40 — dip bot run report

*dip28: deeper limit 0.15 (T2 + re-arm) + holds state + safe-timer log, on run 39's config (lone-whale skip 50, ann on, re-arm 0.65, profit lock 1.0/30%)*

> **Verdict:** +5.625 SOL on chain (14.716788 -> 20.341811) in 17 h 31 min (10-30 08:52 -> 10-31 02:23 PKT, stopped by the owner). 351 positions: T2 224 (realized +2.995), re-arm 127 (realized +2.584); realized +6.109, failed-try fees 0.478. Pace about +7.7 SOL/day; peak +6.0 at 00:45, a cold stretch 01:16-01:48 (14 of 15 trades lost, mostly flat -0.02). Live filters (paper): create-slot skip +1.5, age-1 skip +0.8, QUIET +0.6, 10-sec skip +0.1; deeper limit about -0.5 net (blocks -1.0, deeper entries +0.5, noisy +/-0.25 all day: coin flip, off in r41). Log-only on real fills: group risk (new, offline) +0.6, holder clock +0.5, safe sell timer +0.4, bad creator -0.3 (it would have blocked FkaMPDYA +0.28). Paper vs live: T2 live +27% over shadow; re-arm paper overstates 2.2x. Re-arm 200 ms exit +0.5 on paper (about +0.2 live). Creator-holds-big-and-dumps and group-of-2-6%-holders dumps were the big losses; only the landing-guard family catches them. Replay 20 runs: group risk judged at landing +4.27 SOL (15/20 runs), judged at signing -14.35 (needs the on-chain landing check). End check 0 held.

## 4b. Pre-send live (pre-placed buys) — separate from the reactive bot
Buys pre-sent after a big whale buy, before any dump. **Not included in sections 5–11**, which cover only the reactive dip bot. Realized = the engine's PRESEND LIVE REALIZED records (chain txs of each buy and sell; the buy pays the token-account rent). Rent back = that rent returned when the engine closed each pre-send account after the exit (dip19+; before dip19 the sell closed it and the refund sat inside realized). Failed tries = fees of tries that landed and failed (6042 price, deadline, …). Net = realized + rent back − failed tries.
